The right artifact is the test module + helper + risk-register transition — NOT a new rule. User-TRI-1 + dual-Critic + dogfood all confirmed.
- **BCR-1 traceability axis (slice-054 M-add-1 lineage) applied analogously to risk-register-driven slices**: shippability row #56 cites BOTH `slice-056` AND `R-15`, regression-pinned by `test_shippability_row_56_present_and_cites_r15`. The slice-054 SC-NNN trace axis discipline generalized: row cites BOTH the slice number AND the discharge identifier (SC-NNN for backlog-driven, R-NN for risk-register-driven).
- **R-15 part-(b) structural backstop (not just observational)**: the corpus class-closure backstop's whitelist-shrinkage mechanism IS a structural mechanism for R-15 part-(b) retirement. When the slice-034 retrofit removes the whitelist entry AND the corpus has no match, the assertion structurally satisfies part-(b) without waiting for cross-slice observational evidence. Pattern generalizes for any "future slice demonstrably uses the discipline" gate.

### Didn't work

- **R-15 explanatory comment first attempt contained the matched literal-path-RHS substring** — circular self-detection. AC3 + corpus backstop both FAILed at mid-slice smoke on the comment text. Mid-slice smoke gate caught it; quick paraphrase fix. N=1 watch-list class signal (doc-comment-vs-audit-literal self-detection).
- **Corpus backstop's first attempt scanned per-line** — missed the slice-034 multi-line construction at `test_ptffd1_no_false_positive.py:70-71`. Switched to whole-file `re.finditer(text)` + line-number-from-offset. The bug was the per-line iteration, not the regex (`\s*` already spans newlines).
- **Shippability row #56 description first contained literal `|` in `Path | None = None`** — BC-PROJ-7 violation; SCMD-1 audit's naive `inner.split("|")` ignored markdown `\|` escapes; literal pipe shifted columns. First fix attempt with `\|` escape did NOT help (parser is naive). Final fix: reword the prose to avoid the pipe character entirely. N=1 watch-list class signal (BC-PROJ-7 scope clarification: pipes inside backtick-fenced code spans inside Description text).
- **Meta-Critic M-add-1 proposed fix mechanism was wrong at the regex layer**: option (a) "split body AC into `4a./4b./4c.`" would NOT work because TF-1 `_AC_ITEM_RE = r"^\s*(\d+)\.\s+\S"` rejects `4a.`. Builder empirical verification + chose multi-row-per-AC. The underlying finding (M-add-1) remained VALIDATED; the specific implementation recommendation was wrong. N=1 watch-list class signal (meta-Critic's proposed fix mechanism needs Builder empirical verification before adoption — Builder recompute-don't-trust at N≥6 cumulative).

### Pattern

- **Build-time audit-vs-real-artifact gates are the durable structural backstop for the missed-by-dual-Critic class** (slice-037 N≥9): three new N=1 watch-list signals on slice-056 (doc-comment self-detection; BC-PROJ-7 in-code-span; meta-Critic proposed-fix verification) all build-time-reachable + gate-caught. Per slice-037 law: NO Critic dimensions; calibration via watch-list / `/critic-calibrate`.
- **Voluntary-restraint is a tunable knob driven by failure-class recurrence + structural-mechanism availability** (not by severity): slice-054 minted PVFS-1 on a recurring class (pyproject↔VERSION drift, N≥2); slice-056 mints nothing on a class-signal-N=1 R-15 with structural whitelist-shrinkage backstop. Severity (Major in both cases) is the wrong indicator; the question is "is the class recurring AND would a rule add enforcement the existing structure doesn't already provide?". Slice-056 had structural backstop available (whitelist-shrinkage); no rule needed.
- **Helper-with-call-time-globals + monkeypatch is the canonical Python pattern for test-utility-with-injectable-REPO_ROOT**: avoid default-argument capture (`def f(_root=REPO_ROOT)`); avoid closure cell (`from .conftest import REPO_ROOT; f = lambda: REPO_ROOT.joinpath(...)`); use module-globals access at call-time (`REPO_ROOT.joinpath(...)` inline; Python resolves at each call). Reusable pattern for any future test utility that needs to be redirected at a tmp vault.

## Slice 057 (retire-r15-via-slice-034-resolve-slice-dir-retrofit) — 2026-05-21

### Worked

- **slice-056's two-part R-15 retirement gate engineered correctly**: the M-add-2 corpus class-closure backstop's whitelist-shrinkage mechanism fired structurally on Task 2 alone (whitelist → `set()` + zero corpus matches = both halves of the backstop assertion trivially satisfied). No cross-slice observational evidence needed; the design pattern slice-056 invented (per /critique-review M-add-2 ACCEPTED-FIXED) generalized cleanly to its first governed slice. **Pattern: a deferred-with-whitelist mechanism + a dual-direction class-closure assertion (`matches - whitelist` AND `whitelist - matches`) is the canonical structural-witness-for-cross-slice-discipline-propagation idiom**. Reusable for any future "future slice demonstrably uses this helper/discipline" gate.
- **Builder self-catch of TPHD-1 sub-mode (b) carry-over harmonization pre-/critique-review**: caught the design.md §"Validation strategy" stale "two affected tests" → "three" rewrite (post-/critique M1 ACCEPTED-FIXED carry-over) BEFORE launching the meta-Critic. One targeted Edit; saved the dual-Critic stack from spending an EXTEND finding on Builder carry-over noise; kept calibration clean. N+1 to slice-049's "Builder recompute-don't-trust must extend to one's OWN /critique ACCEPTED-FIXED edits" pattern.
- **slice-040 N+1 doctrine catches the canonical first-governed-slice blind spot**: slice-056 minted the corpus class-closure backstop mechanism (a structurally-novel surface combining whitelist-shrinkage + self-skip clause + dual-direction assertion); slice-057 was its first governed slice; the meta-Critic caught m-add-2 specifically (comment-rewrite-could-self-match risk class) that the first-Critic missed because the first-Critic optimized the mechanical pin/changelog surface and under-weighted the novel structural edge (slice-046 reclassification-slice blind-spot pattern applied here to slice-056-just-minted-mechanism class). N+1 to N=11 cumulative across slice-026/029/038/039/046/049/050/051/054/055/056/057. **The doctrine is now a stable structural law for codification-class slices**: budget the meta-Critic as the structural backstop, do NOT add new first-Critic dimensions (slice-037 audit-vs-real-artifact law continues to hold).
- **Retirement-discharge slice class is N=5 stable**: slice-040 R-10 / slice-043 R-6 / slice-045 R-11 / slice-056 R-15-part-(a) / slice-057 R-15-part-(b) all used MEPD-1(b) discharge-by-name against the real META-1 assertion at `test_methodology_changelog.py:136` (NOT precedent analogy alone, per slice-032 false-precedent guard). The classification is now robust enough to be a stable design-time pattern rather than per-slice judgment.
- **22-audit Step 6 sweep + LINT-MOCK + shippability runner 57/57 + methodology suite 792/792 all clean** — design.md §"Step 6 audit-impact analysis" predicted all 22 audits would run clean post-edits, and they did. Pre-finish gate predictions held in practice; the Builder's design-time gate-impact analysis is reliable for methodology-only slices.

### Didn't work

Nothing material. The plan as approved at /build-slice Step 3 executed verbatim with zero design deviations. No "we got lucky" surprises; no half-broken corners; no late-stage fixes. This is consistent with the slice's class (retirement-discharge of a pre-engineered gate) — slice-056 had already done the structural design work, so slice-057's execution was mechanical.

### Pattern

- **Slice-040 N+1 doctrine extends to N=11 cumulative**: codification-class slices that mint fresh discipline rules / structural mechanisms / audit surfaces consistently produce first-Critic blind spots on the N+1 first-governed-slice; the DR-1 meta-Critic is the structural backstop. **For any future codification slice, budget the meta-Critic to catch the novel structural edge that is the slice's raison d'être** — the first-Critic optimizes the mechanical surface and structurally under-weights the novel edge. Do NOT add new first-Critic dimensions for these classes.
- **Retirement-discharge slices' canonical artifact set**: (i) flip `**Status**:` field-line in risk-register.md to `retired` (RR-1 parses status SOLELY from this field — adding only `**Retired**:` silently false-greens; per slice-043 BC-PROJ-6); (ii) append a retirement paragraph at section end with full discharge mechanics (mechanism citation, META-1 verification, precedent corroboration); (iii) preserve all prior prose verbatim per slice-040 R-10 retirement-precedent. The 5-slice N=5 same-class pattern is now stable enough to template.
- **Voluntary-restraint discipline is the right knob for class-signal-N=1 risks with structural-mechanism backstop available**: slice-040/046/050/052/055/056/057 N=7 cumulative on codification slices. When a risk's retirement-discharge mechanism is already in the repo via a prior slice's helper/test/risk-register-entry AND the slice's state-transition is parametric within an already-shipped rule, no new rule / ADR / methodology-changelog entry / VERSION bump is needed. The classification holds without methodology surface bloat.
- **Builder guard-rail discipline for novel-structural-mechanism slices** (slice-057 m-add-2 catch): when a slice's central mechanism is a corpus-backstop or self-matching-regex surface, explicitly include a Builder guard-rail in design.md §"What's new" naming (a) regex match shape that would trigger a self-match, (b) self-skip clause's exact scope (file-only vs file-and-dependents), (c) safe alternatives if an in-comment example is needed, (d) prose-mention-is-safe-by-construction guarantee. The N+1 first-governed-slice for any future regex-backstop family member benefits from this checklist.

## Slice 058 (add-install-wakeup-prompt-guardrail) — 2026-05-22

### Worked

- **The `/query-design` → `/slice` handoff produced a clean, well-scoped slice.** The slice originated from a `/query-design` conversation where the user weighed a real design tradeoff (INSTALL.md global-append vs per-project `/triage`+`/adopt` CLAUDE.md generation); that deliberation pre-resolved the placement question, so `/design-slice` could decide it cleanly via ADR-057 (citing ADR-049's established global-only install posture) with no user round-trip. Pattern: a `/query-design` session that surfaces a concrete design tradeoff is a high-quality slice feeder — the design decision arrives half-made and well-reasoned.
- **Test-first prose-existence pins + a genuine mid-slice smoke gate.** Writing the 3 pins BEFORE the INSTALL.md edit and confirming all 3 FAIL pre-edit (then 3 PASS post-edit) gave a real, non-tautological FAIL→PASS contrast — the slice-045 `test_install_md_correctness.py` pattern reused cleanly.
- **Dual-Critic stack: first Critic 6/6 VALID + 0 false alarms; meta-Critic EXTEND added 2 genuine misses.** The meta-Critic caught a recipe-completeness gap (step placement) + a test/text-coupling gap (pinning a still-"Draft" block) — both dimensions the first Critic structurally under-weighted. N≥11 cumulative zero-false-alarm on codification slices; DR-1 earning its keep exactly as designed.
- **18 pre-finish audits + drift-check + shippability 58/58 all green first-run.** Zero design deviations; the plan executed verbatim.

### Didn't work

- **slice-045's INSTALL.md version-literal fix was itself drift-prone.** slice-045 fixed a stale `v0.20.0` by substituting a hard `v0.54.0` literal — which re-drifted across the v0.48–v0.54 bumps and was stale again by slice-058 (N=2). The first Critic caught it as B2. Lesson: fixing a stale literal by *refreshing* the literal just resets the drift clock; the only durable fix is to remove the literal and reference `VERSION`. slice-058's reword leaves INSTALL.md with zero version literals.
- **A test-pinned artifact was left labelled "Draft" in design.md.** The meta-Critic's M-add-1 caught that AC2's discrete asserts pinned a block design.md still called a "Draft" — a latent test/text divergence. Resolved by freezing the block + naming verbatim anchors. The first Critic's M2 demanded discrete asserts but did not close the loop on freezing the text those asserts bind to.

### Pattern

- **Recipe / methodology docs must reference `VERSION` dynamically — never hard-code a version literal.** Hard literals re-drift every bump (N=2 in INSTALL.md: v0.20.0 → v0.54.0). When fixing a stale version literal, remove it; don't refresh it.
- **For a content slice where a test pins prose, freeze the prose at design time + name the verbatim test anchors.** A "Draft"-labelled artifact under a downstream pin is a latent divergence — the canonical-phrase discipline FBCD-1 applies to test-function names extends to test-pinned prose blocks.
- **A new step in a numbered procedure needs an explicit design-time placement decision, including the renumber-vs-append call.** Check for letter-referenced cross-refs first: INSTALL.md's "Source independence" section references Step 3f/3g by letter, which made append-as-`3h` strictly cheaper (zero cross-ref churn) than insert-as-`3e` + renumber.
- **The meta-Critic's structural niche on codification slices is the "completeness" dimensions the first Critic skips** — recipe/procedure completeness, test/text freeze coupling. Consistent with the slice-040 N+1 doctrine: budget the meta-Critic as the backstop, don't add first-Critic dimensions.

## Slice 059 (add-tools-package-version-gate) — 2026-05-23

### Worked

- **An empirical Critic attack caught a load-bearing false claim no amount of reading would have.** The original design rested on "`importlib.metadata.version()` reads the venv copy because no `.dist-info` exists in-tree." The first Critic *executed* the read against the real repo+venv (APED-1: rewrote the in-repo `ai_sdlc_tools.egg-info/PKG-INFO` to `9.9.9`, observed `version()` return `9.9.9`) and proved it false — the in-repo egg-info shadows the venv `.dist-info` on `sys.path`. Without B1 the slice would have shipped a tautologically-green gate. The Builder re-verified the corrected `sysconfig.purelib`-scoped mechanism before claiming ACCEPTED-FIXED.
- **The dual-Critic stack on a codification slice: first Critic BLOCKED (3B/3M/2m, all VALID), meta-Critic EXTEND (+2 Minor missed), all 10 ACCEPTED-FIXED, TRI-1 CLEAN.** Zero false alarms, zero severity miscalibrations. The N+1 doctrine held — the first Critic got the novel mechanism edge (B1) directly.
- **TVFS-1 self-gated its own slice.** At slice-059's own Step 6 the venv was `0.62.0` vs VERSION `0.63.0`; the named `pip install --upgrade .` build step refreshed it; TVFS-1 then exited 0 synced — the bootstrap working exactly as ADR-052's bootstrap-honesty precedent predicted.
- **Full methodology suite 807/807, 14 Step 6 audits green, shippability 59/59, /drift-check CLEAN.**

### Didn't work

- **The design enumerated three registration touchpoints for a new `tools/` module; there are five.** `INSTALL.md`'s test-enforced tool-count literal and `test_utf8_stdout_regression.py`'s cp1252-coverage `_ROOT_ONLY_TOOLS` list were missed by the design AND by both Critics — they surfaced only as methodology-suite FAILures on the first full-suite run at build Step 6. Fixed in-band; build-time-reachable and gate-caught at intended latency.

### Pattern

- **Adding a `tools/` module touches five inventories**: `plugin.yaml`, `install_audit.py` `_CANONICAL_TOOLS`, `architecture/shippability.md`, `INSTALL.md`'s tool-count literal, `test_utf8_stdout_regression.py`'s cp1252-coverage list. Enumerate all five at design time. A `/critic-calibrate` watch-list candidate (build-time-reachable per the slice-037 law → not a first-Critic dimension, not a BC-1 promotion at N=1).
- **When a slice's correctness rests on a runtime-resolution claim ("mechanism M reads source X"), the Critic must EXECUTE M, not reason about it.** Reading the design would have accepted B1's falsehood; running `importlib.metadata` exposed it.
- **TVFS-1 closes the version-bearing-leg gate family** — PMI-1 (`plugin.yaml`) / PVFS-1 (`pyproject.toml`) / AVFS-1 (installed `ai-sdlc-VERSION`) / MCFS-1 (installed changelog) / TVFS-1 (installed pip distribution). Every version-bearing surface is now gated.

## Slice 060 (add-code-review-skill) — 2026-05-23

### Worked
- **Bootstrap-discharge per slice-026 / slice-027 precedent (N=3)** scaled cleanly to CRSI-1: when a slice authors a new methodology surface that requires harness session-load registration (a new subagent), the structural discharge (agent file installed + skill installed + chain wired + drift guards + tests + shippability row) IS the proof; the first-real-exercise lands at the NEXT slice's auto-advance edge.
- **Dual-Critic + DR-1 caught all 14 first-Critic + 3 meta-Critic findings at correct severity** (5B/5M/4m + M-add-1/M-add-2/m-add-1). Zero false-alarms across both layers. Meta-Critic precision held on Builder-fix-prose surfaces (slice-049 lineage N+1).
- **5-part PMI-1 atomic bump worked first-try** after the B2 PVFS-1 leg correction; all 5 forward-sync gates (PMI-1 + PVFS-1 + AVFS-1 + MCFS-1 + TVFS-1) exit 0 in lock-step.
- **Walking-skeleton WS-1 6/6 EXERCISED** validates the "every architectural layer exercised end-to-end" Cockburn discipline applies cleanly to methodology-internal slices (not just user-facing features).
- **OSDG-1 + CAD-1 family-add pattern (slice-049/051 lineage) extends cleanly to slice-060** without introducing a new byte-equality comparator (R-5 retirement / EOL-DRIFT-1 preserved).

### Didn't work
- **The first-Critic + meta-Critic dual stack MISSED M1 (diff-resolution triple-dot working-tree gap)** — both layers READ the SKILL.md prose but did not EXECUTE the proposed `git diff "$base"...HEAD` mechanism against the real post-/build-slice git state. Surfaced only at Phase C self-dogfood when the empty `git diff` output triggered the `NO-CODE-CHANGES` branch on a slice that obviously HAS code changes.
- **M2 (harness session-load timing for new subagent registration)** — both Critic layers missed it because the Critic dimensions don't currently cover Claude Code platform-behavior constraints. Surfaced at Phase C agent-spawn attempt with "Agent type 'code-review' not found".
- **Bulk `sed` substitution on the TF-1 plan over-replaced** the Walking-skeleton table's EXERCISED tokens with PASSING. WS-1 audit caught it; targeted `re.search` fix applied.

### Pattern

**APED-1 (Audit-Parse-Rule Empirical-Execution Discipline) extends from `tools/*.py` parse rules to `skills/*/SKILL.md` prose-as-executable-contract.** When a slice authors a new SKILL.md with a runtime-resolution claim ("the skill resolves the diff via mechanism X"), the Critic must EXECUTE the proposed mechanism (Bash the `git diff` command from the cited prose) against the real post-/build-slice git state, not just read the prose. The slice-037 APED-1 lineage applies to SKILL-prose with the same force as audit-tool-prose. N=2 distinct prose surfaces now exhibit this class.

**The "design correction is itself an unguarded adversarial surface" class is at N=5+ cumulative** (slice-032 / slice-042 / slice-049 / slice-058 / slice-060). Past the promotion threshold for a Critic prompt dimension update via `/critic-calibrate`. Strong recommendation to run `/critic-calibrate` in the next 1-2 slices to formalize.

**Bootstrap-discharge pattern is now a stable N=3 mechanism** (slice-026 CRP-1, slice-027 PCA-1, slice-060 CRSI-1). For any future slice that authors a new methodology surface requiring harness session-load registration, structural discharge (file existence + tests + drift guards + shippability row) IS the proof; first-real-exercise lands at next slice's auto-advance edge. This pattern is now durable enough to codify.

## Slice 061 (fix-install-python-detection-and-prompt-fallback) — 2026-05-23

### Worked

- **Conditional confirm-then-auto-invoke /repro edge** (slice-046 ADR-048): the BFRD-1 confirm gate distilled the user's bug report into a one-line description, presented via structured options (Confirm / Modify / Not a bug — cancel), and on Confirm auto-invoked /repro exactly once. No mechanical hand-off; no manual /repro invocation; bug-fix slice started cleanly with a failing repro test in hand.
- **Dual-Critic stack on a defect-repair slice**: 12 first-Critic findings + 2 meta-Critic missed findings, all VALIDATED, ZERO false-alarms (N=12 cumulative on codification slices 046/048/050/051/052/053/054/055/056/057/058/061). The dual stack continues to be precision-correct on defect-repair-class slices where the slice is small and the surfaces well-scoped.
- **slice-055 user-approved-deferral precedent extended to N=2** (slice-061): when shippability dogfood surfaces a PRE-EXISTING prior-slice defect, the right disposition is user-approved deferral via structured-options at /build-slice Step 6 + slice-N+1 nomination for the structural fix. Pattern is durable enough to codify as a documented disposition shape for /build-slice's pre-finish gate.
- **/code-review v1's advisory-only + graceful-degradation contract**: when AGENT-UNSPAWNABLE fired at the slice-061 auto-advance hop, the SKILL.md's documented error semantics + structural-coverage delegation (per slice-061 TRI-1 M-add-1 Option (b)) meant the slice could complete cleanly without /code-review running. The walking-skeleton v1 design held up — advisory-only is the right scope for the first governed slice.

### Didn't work

- **First-Critic + meta-Critic both missed a sibling-instance of the same class their M5 finding addressed.** The Python-version-floor literal at INSTALL.md L73 (`Python 3.10 or newer`) carried a hardcoded `3.10` even though the M5 fix specified dynamic-pyproject-grep for the path-validation surface. BC-PROJ-11 grep at /build-slice Step 6 caught it cleanly at intended latency, but the design-time scope (Critic stack) missed the sibling-coverage. N=1 watch-list candidate for `/critic-calibrate`. If N=2 emerges across future slices, promote to a Critic-prompt dimension extension: "when fixing a hardcoded literal at one surface, scan the entire artifact for sibling instances of the same class in adjacent prose."

- **Stale conversation-start git-status snapshot footgun** (Claude-side, separate from the slice's substance): the conversation-start system context contained a git-status snapshot showing slice-060 as in-progress, but the user had committed + merged slice-060 BEFORE invoking /slice. The mistake was trusting the stale snapshot instead of running a fresh `git status`. User pushed back ("are we not creating different branches for each slice where no two slices will collide?") — the recovery cost was real (delete stashed test file, undo memory write, restart the slice). Logged as feedback memory `[[verify-git-status-before-declaring-conflict]]`. Long-term mitigation: ALWAYS run a fresh git-status check before gating on branch/slice state — the conversation-start snapshot is stale the instant the user commits, merges, or switches branches.

- **Newly-installed Claude Code subagents not hot-loaded into the running session** (R-18 NEW). Class-signal N=1; recurring for any slice immediately following an agent-shipping slice in the same session. slice-060 shipped /code-review; slice-061 in the same session hit `agent-unspawnable`. Methodology-side mitigation candidate at slice-062 (post-build new-agent warning + structural compensation via existing structural review surfaces).

### Pattern

- **The slice-040 N+1 first-governed-slice doctrine extends to N=12 cumulative** with slice-061's first-governed-slice catch on the slice-060 /code-review M-add-1 (INSTALL.md not in /code-review in-scope list). When a slice mints a new methodology surface (rule, skill, or surface-list), the FIRST downstream slice that exercises it surfaces the boundary conditions the dual-Critic stack predicts only weakly. The DR-1 meta-Critic IS the structural backstop for this class. Pattern is now a stable structural law for codification-class slices.

- **Cross-slice runtime-state interactions are a NEW Critic blind-spot class** (R-18 N=1). Neither first-Critic, meta-Critic, nor code-Critic can reach into runtime-state characteristics of the Claude Code harness itself (agent registry, session-load behavior, tool registry). Build-time methodology-side warnings + risk-register tracking are the right structural tooling for this class. If N=2 emerges, consider a /critic-calibrate dimension extension.

- **Sibling-coverage gap on multi-surface fixes** (BC-PROJ-11 N=1 watch-list). When a Critic finding identifies a class-of-defect at one surface, the SAME class often manifests at sibling surfaces in the same artifact. The fix-prose addresses the named surface; the sibling surfaces silently retain the class instance. BC-PROJ-11 was the audit-time backstop for slice-061's specific case; the design-time discipline (extend the M5 fix scope to the entire artifact, not just the named site) is the cheaper move. Track if N=2 emerges.

- **The "voluntary-restraint at codification-class slices" discipline extends to N=8 cumulative** (slice-037/046/050/052/055/056/057/061): when (a) the rule's behavior is already in the repo via a prior slice, (b) the structural-mechanism backstop is already in place, AND (c) the slice's state-transition is parametric within an already-shipped rule — no new rule / ADR / methodology-changelog entry / VERSION bump is needed. Slice-061's R-16 born-retired discharge + R-17/R-18 new mitigating entries follow the slice-045 R-11 born-retired precedent + slice-057 R-15 Notes-paragraph pattern; MEPD-1(b) discharge-by-name verified against META-1 assertion at `tests/methodology/test_methodology_changelog.py:136`.

## Slice 062 (extend-r15-corpus-class-closure-scope) — 2026-05-23

### Worked

- **Helper extraction as per-line equivalent refactor** — the slice-056 corpus class-closure backstop body factored cleanly into `_scan_corpus_for_r15_literals(corpus_dir)` + `_assert_corpus_clean(matches, label)` helpers without behavior change at the methodology-corpus call site. Mid-slice smoke gate produced the verbatim predicted FAIL signature naming exactly the predicted file+line. The walk-proof discipline genuinely falls out structurally via pytest collection — each per-corpus test's existence + pass IS the proof its corpus is walked.
- **Lazy `_resolve_slice_dir(60)` repoint inside the test function** — the cross-package import `from tests.methodology.conftest import _resolve_slice_dir` from `tests/skills/code_review/test_code_review_skill.py` resolves cleanly under pytest's `pythonpath = .` (verified at /design-slice + /build-slice Phase B + Phase D shippability runner). Lazy resolution gives cleaner test-time AssertionError surface vs module-import-time collection-error class.
- **5-part PMI-1 bump propagation discipline at multiple sites** — when the first Critic caught B1 (4-part vs 5-part), the fix-block applied the correction at 7 sites (design.md What's-new + Components-touched + Phase C + Phase D + CSP-1 row + ADR-060 Consequences + mission-brief Pre-finish gate); empirically re-verified at Phase D (PVFS-1 / AVFS-1 / MCFS-1 / TVFS-1 / PMI-1 all exit 0).
- **MEPD-1 Inclusion-heuristic firing on scope-extension precedent (N=6 cumulative)** — slice-049/050/051/057/058/059/062 confirm that drift-guard family scope-extension warrants a methodology-changelog entry + ADR + 4/5-part PMI-1 bump + shippability row, even when no new RULE-ID is minted (extends-not-mints shape; ADR-060 follows the ADR-053 OSDG-1-member-addition naming-class-peer shape verbatim).
- **/critique-review meta-Critic specialization for empirical-audit-execution** — meta-Critic ran `tools.test_first_audit --strict-pre-finish` empirically against the post-/critique-fix-block mission-brief and reproduced 2 violations the first Critic structurally couldn't catch (cross-document table-shape harmonization without empirical audit re-run). This is the meta-Critic working as designed for the slice-022 RSAD-1 self-violation class extending to dual-Critic stack same-fix-block edits.

### Didn't work

- **/critique m3 ACCEPTED-FIXED fix-block edit introduced /critique-review M-add-1 (Blocker)** — the 4-row → 7-row TF-1 plan harmonization copied the design.md table verbatim, including multi-AC labels `"1, 3"` + `"(audit-trace)"` markers that `tools/test_first_audit.py:_normalize_ac_label` rejects → 2 `ac-without-row` violations on AC#3 + AC#4. N=2 slice-040 N+1 doctrine recurrence on the TF-1-multi-AC-label sub-class (slice-056 /critique-review M-add-1 + slice-062 /critique-review M-add-1) + N=2 slice-022 RSAD-1 self-violation pattern extending to dual-Critic stack same-fix-block edits. Meta-Critic empirically caught it; required a second fix-block at /critique-review to re-harmonize. **Lesson**: the first Critic's table-shape verification is structurally too weak for audit-rejection-of-its-own-output cases; the meta-Critic IS the structural backstop, and that's working correctly — but if N=3 emerges, promote to a /critic-calibrate-route Critic-prompt dimension extension.
- **ADR-060 §Decision paragraph 1 fabricated lessons-learned.md citation** — the original draft quoted "regex pattern needs a self-test that the walker actually visits the corpus" attributed to `architecture/lessons-learned.md`; meta-Critic m1 grep-verified the phrase doesn't exist anywhere in the repo. Cockburn evidence-traceability discipline violated; paraphrase replacement landed with explicit disclaimer.
- **Cross-document option-enumeration incoherence** — 3 schemes drifted across ADR-060 (numerals 1-4), design.md (option B/A), mission-brief (a)/(b)/(c); chosen ADR option 3 literally absent from mission-brief enumeration. Meta-Critic M-add-2 caught it. No audit guards cross-document option-label coherence — relies on Critic stack catching prose-coherence drift.
- **/code-review AGENT-UNSPAWNABLE R-18 recurrence (N=2 cumulative)** — predicted by slice-061 reflection L82; confirmed empirically. The `/code-review` agent is not hot-loaded into a session that predates slice-060's ship of the agent install. User-ratified deferral per slice-061 precedent; slice-063+ active R-18 mitigation nomination.

### Pattern

- **N=12 cumulative confirmation of the slice-040 N+1 first-governed-slice doctrine on the slice-022 RSAD-1 self-violation axis**: discipline rule minted in slice N is blind-spot in slice N+1; the dual-Critic stack's meta-Critic IS the structural backstop for the empirical-audit-execution-on-fix-block-edits class. Per slice-037 audit-vs-real-artifact law (N≥10 cumulative): NO Critic dimensions or BC-1 rules for build-time-reachable classes — the existing gates ARE the structural backstop. The cure for missed classes at this level is /critic-calibrate periodic review (watch-list N=2 → promote at N=3), not new Critic dimensions or BC-1 rules.

- **Extends-not-mints vs voluntary-restraint discipline distinction (cumulative N=6 / N=8 respectively)**: when a slice substantively EXTENDS an existing rule's coverage (drift-guard family member-addition, audit-scope-extension, etc.), it warrants Inclusion-heuristic firing (methodology-changelog entry + ADR + 4/5-part PMI-1 bump + shippability row + extends-not-mints ADR shape per ADR-053 precedent). When a slice DISCHARGES a retirement gate (part-(b) closure / retirement-discharge) on an already-shipped rule with structural-mechanism backstop already in place, it warrants voluntary-restraint (no new rule / ADR / methodology-changelog entry / VERSION bump). Both are tunable knobs driven by what the slice substantively does, not by slice severity. The discipline distinguishes by intent: extending coverage vs discharging a gate.

- **Builder mid-design self-correction without /critique invocation IS a viable lightweight feedback path** (N=2 cumulative with slice-061's BFRD-1 confirm-then-auto-invoke precedent). If Builder catches own /slice-time misreading during /design-slice grounding (e.g., re-reading the slice-061 reflection wording correctly), correct in the same fix block — don't carry forward stale claims into /critique. Slice-062 corrected mission-brief frontmatter L5 "Risk retired" + AC#4 wording inline at /design-slice time before /critique fired. Class: lightweight self-feedback within the same skill invocation; appropriate for /slice-time misreadings caught during /design-slice's deeper grounding.


- **The B1 falsifier class (`git diff <base>...HEAD` is commit-vs-commit only at /build-slice end) extends to N=2 cumulative across surfaces** (slice-063 NAW-1 retired it on its own surface; /code-review SKILL.md v1 still carries it on its own diff resolution). The union-of-three-sources fix (`git diff --diff-filter=A {base}` working-tree-vs-base + `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard` untracked + `git diff --diff-filter=A {base}...HEAD` commits-vs-base, unioned + deduplicated) is the canonical mitigation shape. Pattern: any methodology surface that resolves a slice's code diff via git must handle uncommitted working-tree state because /build-slice doesn't commit (commits at /commit-slice per PCA-1 HARD-STOP terminal contract). Slice-064 ACTIVE nomination: mirror to /code-review. (slice-063)

- **R-18 retirement is methodology-discoverability, NOT runtime guarantee** — NAW-1 surfaces the WARN at /build-slice Step 6 BEFORE the next slice's chain runs. Slice-063's own /code-review did NOT hit AGENT-UNSPAWNABLE this session, demonstrating that R-18's runtime behavior is non-deterministic across Claude Code sessions (slice-061 N=1 + slice-062 N=2 hit it; slice-063 did not). The retirement is structurally sound regardless: methodology-discoverability is the load-bearing property, not runtime-deterministic-prevention. Future R-N risks of the same shape should be retired on the discoverability axis, not the runtime-determinism axis. (slice-063)

- **PTFCD-1 `(extended)` Test-path annotation recurrence is N=2 cumulative** (slice-054 first observed + slice-063 N=2). Discipline: NEVER use parenthesized annotations in TF-1 plan Test-path cells; cite the bare file path; put annotations in Notes section below the table. Build-time TF-1 strict-pre-finish gate IS the structural backstop (slice-037 law). User declined BC-1 promotion at N=2 (recommended option) keeping watch-list discipline; promote to BC-PROJ-NNN if N=3 emerges via /critic-calibrate route. (slice-063)

- **CRSI-1 walking-skeleton v1 value proposition validated at N=1** — code-Critic third-pass added a genuine novel m1 finding on a slice whose dual-Critic stack (first-Critic + meta-Critic) passed CLEAN at TRI-1 (13/13 ACCEPTED-FIXED). The design-stack and code-stack catch structurally-different defect classes (design-stack reviews mission-brief + design.md + ADR prose; code-stack reviews the just-written code diff). Slice-063's m1 finding (dead-code/UX-wart in `_resolve_default_branch` second `try/except FileNotFoundError`) is the kind of small-scope code-level observation the design-stack structurally couldn't reach. CRSI-1 v1's advisory-only walking-skeleton is the right minimum-viable shape; slice-064 v2 (TRI-1 + verdict-block + AI-bloat passes) remains highest-priority next-loop candidate. (slice-063)

- **First-Critic vs meta-Critic emerging specialization signal extends to N=2 cumulative** (slice-062 N=1 + slice-063 N=2): first Critic concentrates on prose drift + cross-doc citation hygiene; meta-Critic concentrates on cross-document enumeration drift + empirical audit re-execution. Specialization is structural (different review surfaces, different attention budgets per cognitive-load discipline) — NOT a Critic-prompt dimension extension. If N=3 confirms at slice-064+, document as stable Critic-stack design observation. (slice-063)

- **MEPD-1 Inclusion-heuristic firing on new-mechanism slice (slice-063 ADR-061 NAW-1 minted) extends to N=6 cumulative on the new-RULE-ID precedent** (slice-049/050/054/059/060/063). The voluntary-restraint-zero-bump-zero-ADR posture (slice-037/046/050/052/055/056/057/061 N=8 cumulative) is RESERVED for retirement-discharge / part-(b) closure shapes; new-mechanism slices like NAW-1 take the full 5-part PMI-1 bump + new ADR + methodology-changelog entry + shippability row + Inclusion-heuristic firing per the discovery-gate axis precedent slice-063 just established. NAW-1 sits adjacent to (NOT extending) the forward-sync family (PMI-1 / PVFS-1 / AVFS-1 / MCFS-1 / TVFS-1). (slice-063)

## Slice 064 (fix-code-review-diff-resolution-falsifier) — 2026-05-23

### Worked

- **B1 falsifier class N=2 cumulative retirement complete** — the canonical union-of-three-sources read mechanism (slice-063 NAW-1 minted) mirrored cleanly onto `/code-review`'s SKILL.md Step 1 surface; ADR-062 captures the sibling-surface application without minting a new RULE-ID (Inclusion-heuristic firing at N=8 cumulative inclusive); slice-064 dogfooded its own fix correctly at /code-review Step 1 (Source (i) WT-vs-base caught all 7 in-scope files; Source (iii) commits-vs-base alone empty — exactly the falsifier class the slice retired).
- **TPHD-1 sub-mode (b) cross-file harmonization at /critique-review fix block** — when meta-Critic M-add-1 surfaced 3 residual stale-path sites the first-Critic B1 fix missed (mission-brief.md L21+L78 + design.md L109), the same-fix-block sweep landed cleanly + the post-fix grep returned 0 hits outside intentional verbatim quotes. Slice-022 RSAD-1 self-violation pattern recurrence caught by the structural backstop (meta-Critic) at intended latency.
- **CRSI-1 walking-skeleton v1 value proposition validated at N=2 cumulative** — code-Critic third-pass found 1 Major + 2 Minor advisory findings on a slice with already-CLEAN dual-Critic stack. Real test-tightness + bash-runtime-correctness defects the design-stack structurally could not reach (design-stack reviewed mission-brief + design.md + ADR-062 prose; code-stack reviewed actual code at the line-level idiom).

### Didn't work

- **First-Critic FALSE-ALARM at slice-064 m3** — first-Critic authored an empirically-tolerated finding (the Test-first HTML-comment R-7 footgun) under a forward-looking "in case it propagates" hedge. The user OVERRIDE was reality-confirmed (TF-1 audit accepts the comment). This is the first FALSE-ALARM on a codification-class slice in the slice-046+ N=15 cumulative window. Future first-Critics should DECLINE to author findings under the "in case it propagates" hedge — that's preserved-for-tests-not-Critic-findings territory; if the empirical evidence already shows the audit tolerates the defect, author the test or watch-list entry, not the Critic finding.
- **First-Critic m1 fix introduced new defect class at meta-Critic M-add-2** — first-Critic m1 bumped the EPGD-1 anchor count from 8 → 9; meta-Critic M-add-2 caught that this created a count drift between ADR-062 ("8-anchor") and design.md ("9-anchor"). Same defect class as slice-062 /critique-review M-add-1 (TF-1 multi-AC-label sub-class same-fix-block self-violation). Slice-022 RSAD-1 self-violation pattern N=3 cumulative on dual-Critic-stack same-fix-block edits.

### Pattern

- **The dual-Critic + code-Critic stack delivers value across the full review depth** — first-Critic catches prose drift + cross-doc citation hygiene + APED-1 empirical-audit-execution at design-time (B1 ran `tools.test_first_audit --strict-pre-finish` empirically); meta-Critic catches cross-document mechanical consistency + same-fix-block-edit-introduced regressions (M-add-1 stale-path sweep + M-add-2 anchor-count drift); code-Critic catches line-level test-tightness + runtime-correctness defects post-build (substring-leak in BFRD-1 repro + default-branch resolver no-programmatic-STOP). Three structural review surfaces; three orthogonal defect classes; cumulative N=14 first-Critic + N=3 meta-Critic missed + N=3 code-Critic findings VALIDATED at correct severity on this slice. The CRSI-1 walking-skeleton v1 thesis (slice-060) is validated at N=2 cumulative. (slice-064)

- **B1 falsifier class retirement pattern: canonical-mechanism-codification + sibling-surface-application** — slice-063 codified the union-of-three-sources read mechanism at NAW-1 (audit-tool surface, Python implementation); slice-064 applied it to `/code-review` SKILL.md (skill prose surface, bash mirror). Both surfaces share the conceptual pattern but use independent implementations (Python subprocess vs Claude-executed bash). Future methodology surfaces needing to observe slice-Step-6 WT state inherit by reference, NOT by code import — sibling-surface application of NAW-1 / ADR-061 / ADR-062. (slice-064)


## Slice 065 (bundle-064-code-critic-cleanup) — 2026-05-23

### Worked

- **Bundled cleanup as advisory-discharge shape** — slice-065 closed slice-064's 3 code-Critic v1 walking-skeleton advisories (M1 substring-leak in BFRD-1 repro + m1 default-branch resolver no-programmatic-STOP + m2 count-assertion forward-looking gap) as a coherent bundled cleanup slice. All 3 advisories cluster on the same SKILL.md + test file surfaces; bundled cleanup is the correct discharge shape per slice-064 reflection's explicit nomination. The canonical pipeline pattern (immediate-prior-slice deferred cleanup → next slice) executes cleanly end-to-end.

- **Canonical `bash_block` scoping idiom retires the M1 substring-leak class structurally** — the 6-line `step_1.find("```bash\n") + step_1.find("\n```", bash_start) + bash_block = step_1[bash_start:bash_end] + assert bash_start >= 0 and bash_end > bash_start` idiom isolates the bash-fenced block content from preamble + post-block prose. Combined with full-filter-shape literal assertion (AC#1 double-redundant tightening) + semantic-invariant count discipline (AC#2 `bash_block.count(filter) == bash_block.count(git_diff)`) + three composable assertions (AC#4 count + brace-group + co-location with `-1` short-circuit safety), the idiom defends against future regression classes the pre-slice-065 substring assertions admitted.

- **TPHD-1 sub-mode (c) Builder self-catch at /build-slice prerequisite caught PTFFD-1 phantom function name** — Builder typo in mission-brief.md TF-1 row #5 (`test_run_catalog_returns_zero_on_all_pass` vs actual `test_main_returns_0_on_all_pass`) survived all 3 Critic passes (design-Critic + meta-Critic + code-Critic) because function-existence verification is structurally backstopped at gate-time, not at Critic-prompt time. Slice-057 N+1 doctrine Builder self-catch precedent applied at pre-flight gate. Division-of-labor: function-existence is a STRUCTURAL gate (TPHD-1 sub-mode (c)), NOT a Critic responsibility.

- **CRSI-1 v1 walking-skeleton stable across slices 063/064/065** — code-Critic finds genuine novel findings on slices with already-CLEAN dual-Critic stack (slice-065: 4 advisory Minors all VALID empirically — m1 SKILL.md L37 sibling coordinate-pin + m2 3× DRY duplication + m3 SKILL.md L69 prose drift + m4 design.md "byte-for-byte" overclaim). The 3-pass review stack (design-Critic + meta-Critic + code-Critic) is empirically validated as STRUCTURALLY DIFFERENTIATED — each pass catches a different defect class.

- **Voluntary-restraint discipline at N=9 cumulative for forward-looking quality cleanup** — slice-065 shipped without methodology-changelog / VERSION / new ADR + declined-in-band all 4 code-Critic advisories per CRSI-1 v1 walking-skeleton + slice-063/064 precedent. The slice-064 ADR-062 "extends-without-minting" in-family precedent is now the canonical authority for slice-N+1 in-family extension dispositions.

### Didn't work

- **Mission-brief TF-1 plan function-name typo** — initial `/slice` mission-brief authoring cited a phantom function name (`test_run_catalog_returns_zero_on_all_pass`) that the dual-Critic + code-Critic stack all missed. Caught only at /build-slice TPHD-1 sub-mode (c) pre-flight gate. The function-name typo did not surface at any Critic layer because function-existence is not a Critic-prompt dimension; it's a structural gate (and correctly so).

- **Design-Critic stack missed third sibling-cell drift at SKILL.md L37** — first-Critic m3 fixed `ADR-061 §Decision L60-67` coordinate-only pin at 2 design.md cite sites; meta-Critic m-add-1 fixed shippability row Description residual; code-Critic m1 then caught third residual at SKILL.md L37 (the very file the slice's AC#3 modifies). The slice-040 N+1 doctrine 3-tranche signature held: no single Critic pass enumerates ALL sibling cells at first-pass; each tranche catches one residual.

- **Design.md "byte-for-byte translation" overclaim** — design.md framed the SKILL.md L42 bash insertion as "mechanically equivalent" + "literal byte-for-byte translation" of NAW-1's Python implementation. Code-Critic m4 empirically refuted via Python-bash message-body diff — the parity is class-level (exit code + stderr stream + kebab class identifier) but NOT byte-for-byte (Python carries rule-ID prefix + remediation hint; bash is condensed). Declined in-band per voluntary-restraint; slice-066+ bundled cleanup nomination.

### Pattern

- **The 3-pass Critic stack catches drift in TRANCHES on first-governed-slice for any new pattern (slice-040 N+1 doctrine 3-tranche structure)** — when slice-N introduces a new mechanism (e.g., section-scoped-substring-assertion pattern at slice-064), slice-N+1 is its first governed slice and the canonical drift manifests as residual sibling-cell or RSAD-1 violations across multiple artifacts. Design-Critic catches drafts; meta-Critic catches first-Critic's same-fix-block-edit-introduced regressions; code-Critic catches third-pass cross-cell drift the design-stack structurally cannot reach because they review prose, not executable code. Each pass catches ONE tranche; do NOT promote any single-pass-missing class to BC-1 (the 3-pass stack IS the structural backstop). Pattern stable at N=15 cumulative slice-040 N+1 doctrine instances + N=3 cumulative CRSI-1 v1 walking-skeleton confirmations.

- **Voluntary-restraint discipline operates uniformly across methodology-event-promotion AND code-Critic advisory dispositions** — both shapes resist mid-slice scope expansion; both shapes nominate next-slice bundled cleanup. CRSI-1 v1 walking-skeleton advisory-only discipline is structurally identical to MEPD-1 voluntary-restraint disposition for methodology-changelog / VERSION / new-ADR promotion. The slice-064 ADR-062 "extends-without-minting" in-family precedent + slice-063/064/065 N=3 cumulative declined-in-band code-Critic precedent are now the canonical authorities for in-family quality-cleanup slice dispositions.

- **First-Critic vs meta-Critic vs code-Critic specialization signal stabilizes at N=3 cumulative — promote to stable Critic-stack design observation if N=4 confirms at slice-066+** — first-Critic on test-assertion drift + cross-doc citation hygiene + APED-1 empirical-audit-execution at design-stage; meta-Critic on cross-document mechanical consistency + same-fix-block-edit-introduced regressions; code-Critic on line-level POSIX/runtime portability + third-pass cross-cell drift + literal-message-body empirical-parity at post-build code-stage. This is a stable structural property of the 3-Critic stack, NOT a Critic-prompt dimension extension.

## Slice 066 (add-worktree-per-slice-discipline) — 2026-05-24

### Worked

- **R-17 candidate-fix-(b) worktree-per-slice closes the WIP-contamination class structurally, not via cleanliness audit.** Per ADR-063 §"Options considered" option 3, the candidate-fix-(a) clean-tree audit was DECLINED as redundant defense-in-depth — option (b) makes the class structurally impossible, so a cleanliness audit becomes a Maginot Line catching a class that can no longer fire. Pattern: when a candidate fix makes the defect class structurally impossible, the parallel defense-in-depth audit is OVER-engineering and should be declined explicitly.
- **N=2 application of slice-022 ADR-020 partial-supersession encoding pattern proves the pattern's reusability.** ADR-019 stays unmodified across N=2 partial supersessions (ADR-020 covers sub-mode (b); ADR-063 covers sub-mode (a)). The append-only rule holds; single `supersedes:` frontmatter slot + body-level scope enumeration is sufficient at N=2 — the pattern doesn't need extension for multi-supersession.
- **Dual-Critic stack continues paying empirically** — 14 first-Critic + 6 meta-Critic = 20 design-level findings caught on slice-066. Most consequential: B1 ADR identifier conflation (BRANCH-1 = ADR-019, not ADR-021 which is utf8-stdout-1) — would have shipped ADR-063 superseding the wrong ADR. Load-bearing design-level catch the structural Step 6 audits couldn't have reached post-build.
- **CRSI-1 v1 walking-skeleton advisory-only discipline held under real adversarial load** — code-Critic returned 0B/2M/4m on a 16-file methodology-surface slice; all 6 findings deferred to slice-067+ bundled cleanup per slice-064/065 advisory-feedback-loop precedent. The chain auto-advanced past the FINDINGS verdict without HALTing, exactly as designed for v1.
- **WS-1 walking-skeleton discipline + the 5-layer architectural enumeration in mission-brief is a clean fit for methodology-surface slices.** All 5 layers (build-slice SKILL.md prose / commit-slice SKILL.md prose / audit module / git plumbing / vault) hit on real git state via 8 audit-module tests on real `tmp_path` + real `git subprocess` fixtures. The slice exercised every layer end-to-end on its own self-test.
- **Idempotent operations remove bootstrap-discharge friction.** Step 5b sub-step 5's `git worktree list --porcelain | grep -E "^branch refs/heads/slice/NNN-<name>$"` → LOG-and-skip-if-absent path covers the slice-066 bootstrap (no worktree) + any future `WORKTREE=skip` slice with the SAME mechanism. No need for three separate `WORKTREE=skip-bootstrap` Events lines per skill (build-slice + validate-slice + commit-slice); the idempotent guard makes the cleanup safe across the bootstrap window with one line.

### Didn't work

- **PowerShell `Write-Host "..." | Out-File ...` writes nothing.** Write-Host outputs to the host, not the pipeline; Out-File receives no input. VERSION + installed ai-sdlc-VERSION became 0-byte files; AVFS-1 passed vacuously (both empty, equal); TVFS-1 caught it. Builder self-catch #9. Cross-shell idiom mismatch the Critic-stack structurally can't reach (Critic reviews the design's bash snippets, not the build-time PowerShell invocations).
- **AC2 prose-pin tests false-PASSed on `git worktree remove` substring.** The existing `skills/commit-slice/SKILL.md:258` diagnostic mentions `git worktree remove` in a `git checkout`-conflict-resolution context (a DIFFERENT context from the new BRANCH-2 teardown). Naive substring checks satisfied the assertion vacuously. Builder self-catch #8. Fix: section-scoping helpers (`_step_5b_merge_section`, `_step_5d_sync_after_pr_section`) extract the discrete sub-section before the substring assert.
- **Cp1252 test fixture encoding broke audit's UTF-8 read.** Test wrote `build-log.md` fixture with `Path.write_text(...)` without explicit encoding; on Windows the default is cp1252; the em-dash (U+2014) became byte 0x97; `_check_escape_hatch` read with `encoding="utf-8"` and failed with `UnicodeDecodeError`. UTF8-STDOUT-1 lineage class but on test FIXTURE files, not audit-tool source. Builder self-catch (Windows cp1252 class N≥7 cumulative).
- **TF-1 plan path verification only checks function-name harmony, not file existence on disk.** Mission-brief.md TF-1 plan rev-1 referenced `test_commit_slice_skill.py` (doesn't exist; split into 3 files) and `test_claude_md_pin.py` (doesn't exist; existing pin at `test_root_claude_md_branch_per_slice_rule.py`). TPHD-1 sub-mode (c) at /build-slice prereq passed because function-names harmonized internally; Glob would have caught the file non-existence.

### Pattern

- **Codification slices in this project consistently produce N≈10 Builder self-catches during build despite N=20+ dual-Critic findings caught at design time.** The two layers are structurally complementary. Dual-Critic reaches the DECLARATIVE layer (design-claim consistency, ADR identifiers, regex grammar pinning, schema completeness, cross-document citation hygiene). Builder self-catches reach the OPERATIONAL layer (Glob-vs-TF-1-plan, cp1252 test fixtures, PowerShell idioms, cross-doc canonical anchors, section-scoping in prose-pin tests, file-existence verification). Future codification slices should budget Phase A + B-prefix sweeps for (a) Glob-verification of every TF-1 plan path, (b) PMI-1 enumeration cross-check against the most-recent slice's canonical 5-part shape, (c) BC-PROJ-10 paired-entry-pin verification at design-time. Slice-022 law "codification slices commit exactly the violation their codified discipline catches" extends to density ≈N=10 per slice (was N=5-8 baseline at slice-020-040; slice-064 N=14; slice-066 N=11).
- **The 3-Critic specialization signal stabilizes at N=4 cumulative.** First-Critic on test-assertion drift + cross-doc citation hygiene + APED-1 empirical-audit-execution at design-stage. Meta-Critic on cross-document mechanical consistency + same-fix-block-edit-introduced regressions + missed-by-first-Critic dimension re-application (N=4 confirms post-slice-064/065 N=3 prediction). Code-Critic on line-level POSIX/runtime portability + Windows path edge cases + third-pass cross-cell drift + empirical-fixture-load-bearing-check at post-build code-stage. Three structurally distinct review windows, neither reachable by the others.
- **Bundled cleanup is now the canonical disposition for code-Critic v1 advisory findings.** Slice-064/065 + slice-066 lineage. The next-slice `slice-NNN-bundle-NNN-code-critic-cleanup` shape (mirrors slice-065) consumes the prior slice's deferred advisory findings under voluntary-restraint discipline (no methodology-changelog entry / no ADR / no VERSION bump per BC-PROJ-10 / Inclusion-heuristic). Slice-067+ inherits this pattern for slice-066's 6 code-Critic findings.
- **WS-1 + ETC-1 audit's R-7-class silent-default-off class is the strongest `/critic-calibrate` proposal target** — N=2 if ETC-1 confirms (TF-1 retired via TFFL-1 at slice-034; WS-1 witnessed N=1 here). Apply slice-034 TFFL-1 pattern verbatim (regex relaxation + value-agnostic field-present malformed branch).

User-ratified WORKTREE=skip workaround used; BRANCH-1 fallback path preserved per ADR-063 §Scope of supersession 4th-surface inheritance. **This is the slice-040 N+1 first-governed-slice doctrine working as designed** — the next slice after a new-mechanism mint is where structurally-novel edges surface. But it does mean BRANCH-2's worktree-per-slice discipline is NOT yet usable for regular slices until slice-068+ reconciles via vault-copy / un-gitignore / symlink.

### Pattern

- **Slice-040 N+1 first-governed-slice doctrine extends to N=16 cumulative on the BRANCH-2 surface**: the slice immediately following a new-mechanism mint is where structurally-novel edges that the mint's design couldn't anticipate surface empirically. Discipline: future first-governed-slices for new structural rules should explicitly probe at /design-slice time "does the rule's design assume git-tracked state that the project's conventions contradict?" — slice-067 would have caught the gitignored-architecture/ conflict at /design-slice instead of /build-slice if this probe had been part of the design discipline.
- **Meta-Critic specialization signal stable at N=3 cumulative on BRANCH-2 first-governed-slice axis** (slice-062 + slice-064 + slice-067): first-Critic catches conceptual + cross-doc citation hygiene; meta-Critic catches Builder fix-block-edit-introduced regressions + empirical audit-re-execution. The two are structurally complementary; collapsing dual-Critic stack to single-Critic would empirically miss the M-add-1/2/3/4 class.
- **CRSI-1 v1 walking-skeleton validated at N=5 cumulative**: design-stack vs code-stack structural differentiation continues to hold. The right next step is CRSI-1 v2 (TRI-1 + verdict-driven block + AI-bloat passes) at slice-068+ once the BRANCH-2 reconciliation + PSQ-2 claim machinery ship.
- **Voluntary-restraint discipline N=9 cumulative is a STABLE pattern** for codification slices that mint new rules: the bundled-cleanup-at-N+1 disposition (slice-064→065 precedent) works AND user-explicit-pick ordering can defer cleanup further. slice-068+ bundled cleanup nomination accumulates: slice-066's 6 advisories + slice-067's 1 advisory = 7-finding bundle.
- **Honest acknowledgement of structural conflicts via WORKTREE=skip is correct discipline**: when a first-governed-slice surfaces a conflict the mint's design didn't anticipate, the right disposition is (a) document the conflict in build-log.md DEVIATION-1, (b) user-ratify via SOAD-1 structured options, (c) use the documented escape-hatch (BRANCH-1 fallback per ADR-063 4th-surface inheritance), (d) nominate the formal reconciliation as a slice-068+ candidate. NOT (e) silently work around it (which would propagate the conflict to future slices).

## Slice 068 (add-vault-root-constant) — 2026-05-25

### Worked

- **Single-seam refactor pattern + leading-underscore-helper convention** scaled cleanly. The 8-site migration applied + the new `tools/_vault_paths.py` leaf module + the new test module + ADR-065 all landed in <1 hour Builder time after a 30-min plan-mode discussion; full pytest 944/0 confirms zero behavior change. Reusable for slice-069's rename (1-line `_DEFAULT` flip).
- **Three-Critic stack complementarity validated at N=2 cumulative** (slice-067 N=1 + slice-068 N=2). Design-Critic catches conceptual + cross-doc citation + missing-cases. Meta-Critic catches Builder fix-block-introduced sibling-cell sweep gaps. Code-Critic catches test-quality + regex-coverage + spec-vs-code drift. Each Critic surfaces distinct, complementary defect classes — collapsing any one would miss real defects.
- **MEPD-1 EXCLUDE posture** for pure mechanical refactors continues to be the right disposition. Slice-068 mints no rule, adds no audit surface, no consumer-propagation obligation → ships at v0.69.0 unchanged with no methodology-changelog entry, no PMI-1 bump. All forward-sync audits (MCFS-1 / AVFS-1 / TVFS-1) stay quiescent because there's nothing to forward-sync. Voluntary-restraint N=10 cumulative.
- **`/critique-review` (DR-1) and `/code-review` (CRSI-1 v1) as paired structural backstops**: meta-Critic caught M-add-1 (sibling-cell sweep gap in Builder fix-block); code-Critic caught M1 (spec-prose-promises-test-not-in-TF-1-plan), m1/m2/m3 (test-body quality gaps); both are now empirically observed as catching distinct classes neither design-Critic alone catches.

### Didn't work

- **N=4 cumulative gitignored-vault-vs-worktree conflict** — for the FOURTH slice (067 + 068 each had two encounters: /slice-time queue-write + /build-slice full-pytest collection), the gitignored `architecture/` (`.gitignore:11` "Local-only AI SDLC vault") didn't propagate to the BRANCH-2 worktree and required either bootstrap workarounds OR explicit `cp -r` DEVIATIONs. This is now a recurring class with structural cost; slice-069's `rename-architecture-to-sdlc-and-track-in-git` (un-gitignore + rename via the new VAULT_ROOT seam) is the structural fix and should be accelerated.
- **Mission-brief rev-1 grep missed a 9th site** (`tools/build_checks_integrity.py:78`) — my original grep regex `["']architecture/|["']architecture\\` matched the file (it showed up in the per-file count = 1) but I didn't read the actual literal context to recognize the `_PROJECT_LIVE_REL = "architecture/build-checks.md"` pattern (semantically identical to slice_queue_writer.py's `_INDEX_MD_REL` private constant). /critique B1 caught it. **Pattern**: when a grep count shows a file with 1 match, READ the actual literal context — don't trust the count alone.
- **/critique fix-block sweep missed mission-brief sibling-cell sites** (M-add-1 two-marker convention asymmetry) — the Builder swept design.md but missed mission-brief AC2 + must-not-defer #2 + Verification-plan row 2. Slice-067 N=3 → slice-068 N=4 cumulative pattern of "Builder applies multi-finding fixes without sweeping all sibling-cell sites." Meta-Critic specialization is now well-established; `/critic-calibrate` proposal queued.
- **Design.md prose promised a test not enumerated in the TF-1 plan** (`test_vault_paths_module_is_leaf` at design.md L25 + mission-brief L54) — neither design-Critic nor meta-Critic flagged this; /code-review M1 caught it post-build. Fresh class for `/critic-calibrate`: design-Critic dimension addition for "every design prose mention of `test_*` function name has a TF-1 row".

### Pattern

- **Three-Critic complementarity is the durable defect-catching architecture** at N=2 cumulative. The three Critics structurally cover: design-Critic = conceptual/citation; meta-Critic = fix-block-sweep gaps; code-Critic = test-quality/regex/cosmetic/spec-vs-code drift. Empirically validated; the slice-062 verdict-driven block extension for /code-review is the natural next step (CRSI-1 v2 candidate).
- **Builder fix-block sweep discipline** (slice-067 N=3 → slice-068 N=4) — when a Critic surfaces a multi-finding fix-block, the Builder MUST sweep ALL sibling-cell sites enumerated by name across mission-brief + design.md + ADRs + tests. Meta-Critic catches gaps. Candidate `/critic-calibrate` proposal: "first-Critic fix dispositions should explicitly enumerate the cross-document sweep surface, not leave it implicit."
- **Gitignored-vault-vs-worktree probe** should become a design-Critic dimension per slice-067 N=3 + slice-068 N=4 cumulative recurrence. At /design-slice for any BRANCH-2 slice that reads from `architecture/`, `diagnose-out/`, or `graphify-out/`, explicitly probe: "does the slice depend on gitignored state that won't propagate to the worktree?"
- **Single-seam refactor → rename slice** pattern is reusable: introduce a seam constant first (this slice), then flip the constant default in a follow-on slice (slice-069). The seam slice's blast radius is bounded by find/replace tooling; the rename slice's blast radius shrinks from O(N migration sites) to O(1 constant + .gitignore + philosophy ADR). Applicable to future "rename a widely-used path constant" scenarios.

## Slice 069 (track-vault-in-git) — 2026-05-25

### Worked

- **N=4 cumulative gitignored-vault-vs-worktree class STRUCTURALLY RETIRED** via ADR-066 vault-in-git philosophy decision. Empirical proof at /build-slice Phase H4: `git worktree add --detach ../slice-069-verify HEAD` propagated 634 files in `architecture/` automatically, no `cp -r` DEVIATION needed.
- **BC-PROJ-8 rule-content supersession via lockstep live + canonical-fixture edit** — BCI-1 dual-source contract from slice-030A paid off cleanly at first revise. Trigger keywords 7-tuple → 6-tuple (drops `"gitignored"`); rule prescription unchanged.
- **STP-1 Sub-form B docstring rationale reframed origin → preference** preserves slice-044 historical anchor while acknowledging post-slice-069 vault is tracked.
- **M5 INCLUDE direction for /code-review** (user-chosen at /build-slice) admits `architecture/slices/*/{build-log,validation,reflection}.md` to /code-review scope via 10 surgical exclusions per leg.
- **PII redaction sweep scoped to `architecture/**`** (per /critique-review M-add-3): 4 patterns, 36 files changed, 141 substitutions, 0/0/0 final residuals; verbatim corpus per ADR-030 preserved.
- **Reversibility taxonomy promoted inline in ADR-066** (NEW section) replaces fabricated "ADR-019 reversibility taxonomy" citation; subsequent ADRs can cite forward.
- **5-part PMI-1 atomic bump 0.69.0 → 0.70.0** completed cleanly with post-bump MCFS-1 + TVFS-1 forward-syncs.
- **3-Critic stack value-validation extends to N=6 cumulative** (slice-063 → slice-069). Code-Critic surfaces 2 Majors + 6 Minors on a slice whose design-Critic + meta-Critic stack passed CLEAN at TRI-1.

### Didn't work

- **M5 INCLUDE direction's `:(exclude)architecture/*.md` pathspec over-matched on first use** — git's default `fnmatch(3)` without `FNM_PATHNAME` makes `*` cross `/` segments. Caught mid-/code-review when the slice's own diff scope didn't include the build-log.md it was supposed to admit. Fix: `:(glob,exclude)architecture/*.md`. **Classic slice-040 N+1 first-governed-slice catch** — mechanism-specific bugs are reachable only by empirical execution of the just-minted mechanism.
- **Bash-quote silent backslash-stripping in Python regex character classes embedded in heredocs** — both `\\` in double-quoted and single-quoted bash heredocs pass Python source through a JSON-decode + bash-tokenize pipeline that strips one backslash. Fix: construct regex via `chr(92)*2` to avoid the bash-escape interpretation chain.
- **`git worktree add <branch>` collision when branch is checked out elsewhere** — fix: `--detach HEAD`. Not yet codified in `tools/branch_workflow_audit.py` docstring; slice-070+ bundle nomination.
- **`_SECRET_PATTERNS` count drift "9" vs actual "10" across 3+ artifacts** (code-Critic M1) — methodology-changelog v0.70.0 + ADR-066 + mission-brief AC4 all say "9 patterns"; production source code has 10. Build-log L70 + validation.md correctly recorded "10". Slice-050 BC-PROJ-9 inventory-count class N=2 cumulative.
- **MEPD-1 INCLUDE posture declared but no `test_v_0_70_0_*` paired-pin tests** (code-Critic M2) — BC-PROJ-10 paired-pin discipline violation. Every prior INCLUDE-posture slice shipped both. BC-PROJ-10's keyword-match classifier doesn't audit paired-pin existence — RSAD-1 self-application gap. Shippability row #69 added in this /reflect Step 5.3; paired-pin tests deferred to slice-070+ bundle.

### Pattern

- **Methodology-revision slices that mint a new mechanism** (pathspec / regex / git-mechanic / audit-rule) have a structural N+1 first-governed-slice catch surface at the slice's own /code-review hop — NOT at design-stage. /critic-calibrate signal: /build-slice should perform empirical execution of any minted mechanism on a known input before declaring done. Similar to APED-1 empirical-execution discipline at /critique for changed-rule batteries.
- **Count claims in design/ADR/mission-brief prose** need design-Critic hand-verification against production source code (`grep -c` or equivalent). Slice-050 BC-PROJ-9 inventory-count class N=2 cumulative — propose /critique dimension at N=3.
- **MEPD-1 INCLUDE posture triggers BC-PROJ-10 paired-pin obligation** that BC-PROJ-10's keyword-match classifier doesn't structurally verify. Design-Critic should EXECUTE an empirical scan of prior INCLUDE-posture slices' artifact inventory (paired-pin tests + shippability row presence) and demand the same. /critic-calibrate signal N=1.
- **Bundled-cleanup nomination accumulating across N=4 cumulative slices**: slice-066 (6 advisories) + slice-067 (1) + slice-068 (4) + slice-069 (8) = 19-finding backlog. Voluntary-restraint discipline holds; the bundled-cleanup slice is now substantial enough to warrant explicit /slice scope decision.
- **Bash-heredoc Python source with backslash patterns** is a Builder-tool-discipline class: silent regex character-class collapse via JSON-decode + bash-tokenize. Fix via `chr(92)*2` or separate `.py` file invocation. /critic-calibrate watch-list N=1.
- **ADR §Reversibility prose creates BC-1 BC-GLOBAL-2 false-positive surface** that M5 INCLUDE direction further enlarges (build-log §Discovered prose now in /code-review scope). BC-1 keyword-trigger model needs discrimination between prose-discussion vs code-automation. /critic-calibrate watch-list N=1.
- **`/critic-calibrate` probe narrowing post-slice-069**: gitignored-vault probe scope narrows from `architecture/` (now tracked) to `diagnose-out/` + `graphify-out/` (still gitignored).

## Slice 070 (fix-psq-1-blast-radius-dict-leak) — 2026-05-26

### Worked

Real distinct defect classes the design-Critic stack structurally cannot reach.
- **BCR-1 round-trip closure (SC-027) landed cleanly**: `**Closes:** SC-027` sentinel in mission-brief.md → `**Addressed:** slice-070-fix-psq-1-blast-radius-dict-leak on 2026-05-26` line appended after SC-027's Evidence sub-list, before SC-028 header.
- **Switch-commit-switch-worktree pattern for vault-in-git pre-build artifacts**: user-ratified at /build-slice prereq trip-up — this is the new canonical post-slice-069 pattern for first-time-post-vault-in-git slices.

### Didn't work

Surfaced at /build-slice Phase D via empirical execution. **Pattern**: design-Critic stack structurally cannot anticipate regex blind-spots on actual rendered content without empirical execution.
- **TPHD-1 sub-mode (a) cross-doc harmonization gap N=5 cumulative recurrence** (slice-062/064/067/070-meta-Critic/070-build-time): the first-Critic fix-block sweep keeps missing peripheral prose anchors. Meta-Critic catches reliably (per slice-067/070 meta-Critic M-add-3). Pattern is now structural; warrants /critic-calibrate proposal.

The slice's BCR-1 round-trip closure had to be manually replicated in main tree's diagnose-out/ because the worktree's edit doesn't propagate via merge.

### Pattern

- **For any slice that mints or modifies a parse-rule / regex / glob / pathspec, perform empirical execution against actual production data BEFORE declaring /critique done** — extends APED-1's audit-parse-rule discipline to regex/glob/pathspec rules more broadly. The N+1 first-governed-slice catch surface is otherwise at /build-slice via test execution (later than ideal). /critic-calibrate signal N=1 (slice-070 AC#3 dotfile blind-spot); promote if 2nd recurrence.
- **The bundle-cleanup slice nomination at slice-071 is structurally overdue** — 30 code-Critic advisory findings accumulated across slice-066/067/068/069/070. Voluntary-restraint discipline holds (slice-064/065/066/067/068/069/070 N=11 cumulative) but the cleanup slice itself is now LARGE. Recommend explicit /slice scope-check (possibly split into code-quality refactors vs spec-prose corrections).
- **Post-vault-in-git BRANCH-2 + slice-pipeline-artifacts interaction needs codification**: every future first-post-slice-069 slice will trip BRANCH-2 prereq rule 4 "STOP if dirty" because /slice → /critique-review creates tracked artifacts on master tree before /build-slice creates the worktree. The switch-commit-switch-worktree pattern is the documented disposition; needs SKILL.md codification.
- **Builder fix-block prose-honesty discipline**: when deferring a finding, credit the defect's provenance accurately. "Not a defect this slice introduced" is the wrong framing when the slice ships the code that introduces the defect class.

## Slice 071 (bundle-066-to-070-code-critic-cleanup) — 2026-05-26

### Worked

- **Per-finding disposition table as design's load-bearing artifact** enables tractable 31-finding bundled cleanup at 10× slice-065 reference-precedent scale. All 28 FIXes + 3 non-FIX closure dispositions (1 DOCUMENT-AS-DESIGNED + 1 ALREADY-FIXED-AT-/reflect + 1 honest DEFER-AGAIN) landed without scope expansion; mid-slice smoke + final pre-finish gate both clean.
- **Per-cluster paired build phases** (M-add-2 fix): each cluster's source-side FIX + paired regression-pin test land in the same commit. Removes test-first-by-other-name RSAD-1 anti-pattern; tests are verifiable as load-bearing at code review time without requiring sequential RED-then-GREEN cycling.
- **APED-1 conformance (empirical regex execution against actual data BEFORE declaring done)** caught the broken `_PATH_SHAPED_RE` regex at /critique B1 — would have inverted slice-070 M5's intent inside the very M2 fix. N=2 cumulative; strongest /critic-calibrate active nomination.
- **Builder fix-block prose-honesty propagation** (slice-070 reflection lesson → slice-071 enforcement): /critique M2 caught slice-069 m6 disposition-rename anti-pattern; Builder accepted honest DEFER-AGAIN = 1 count in-band. Cross-slice methodology-lesson transmission worked.

- **First-attempt mid-slice smoke gate failed** on `test_bcr_1_sc054_round_trip_inputs_invariant` because gitignored `diagnose-out/backlog.md` was absent from worktree. N=6 cumulative cp-r tax. User-flagged "we need a better solution"; promoted to R-20 risk-register entry with 4 candidate fix classes; slice-072+ highest-priority structural-fix nominee.
- **Disposition-rename anti-pattern was almost shipped** as "ACKNOWLEDGED-NO-FIX" / "zero DEFER-AGAIN" framing pre-Critic. - **Initial sentinel-test assertions did NOT match the proposed docstring prose** (M-add-1 catch).
